tamiḻakamum cīṉāvum koṇṭa palavakit toṭarpukaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ, cīṉāviṉ antantak kālattu araca vamicāvaḷikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi, tamiḻakattu maṉṉarkaḷ, taṅkaḷuṭiya aṉpiyum mariyātiyiyum terivik kiṉṟa muṟiyil aṉuppivanta poruḷkaḷi, cīṉa cakravartti kaḷukkuc celuttappaṭṭa kāṇikkikaḷ eṉṟē collikkoṇṭu pōkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl intac cīṉa varalāṟukaḷē, pallavar kālattil aracāṅkattiṉ cārpākac ceṉṟa tūtu kōṣṭiyiṉar, cīṉa aracaviyil mikavum kuravamāka naṭattappaṭṭaṉareṉṟum, ivarkaḷukku rājāṅka viruntukaḷ pala naṭipeṟṟaṉaveṉṟum, ivarkaḷ tāy nāṭṭukkut tirumpukiṟapōtu, matippuḷḷa anēka poruḷkaḷi aṉpaḷippākap peṟṟārkaḷeṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. cōḻar āṭciyiṉ pōtu ceṉṟa tūtu kōṣṭiyiṉar, cīṉa aracaviyil. vaḻakkamāṉa kuravattuṭaṉ upacarikkappaṭavilli yeṉṟum, itaṟkuk kāraṇam, cōḻar āṭciyiṉ  appoḻutiya māṭcimi paṟṟic cīṉa maṉṉarkaḷukkut teriyātatutāṉeṉṟum collappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. iviyellām eppaṭi iruntapōtilum, cīṉāvil, potuvāka intiyarkaḷuṭiya, ciṟappākat tamiḻar kaḷuṭiya nuṇṇaṟivu, toḻil tiṟami, moḻip pulami mutaliya vaṟṟiṟkup  perumatippu iruntu vantateṉpatu maṟukka muṭiyāta uṇmi.
iṉṉoru viṣayam, potuvāka intiyāvukkum, ciṟap pākat tamiḻakattukkum cīṉāvukkumiṭiyē, aracāṅka tōraṇi yilō, taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyilō pōy vantu koṇṭiruntavar kaḷiṟ perum pālōr viyāpāra apiviruttiyi mukkiya nōkka mākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. viyāpārattiyoṭṭiyē vēṟuvaki yāṉa toṭarpukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
ivaṟṟuḷ mutalāvatu matat toṭarpu. putta matap piracāra nimittam vaṭa intiyāviliruntu palar cīṉāvukkuc ceṉṟatu pōl, tamiḻakattiliruntum palar ceṉṟirukkiṟārkaḷ. oru cilarip paṟṟi maṭṭum iṅkup piratāpippōm.
kāñcipurattil āṇṭa pallava maṉṉarkaḷil oruvaṉuṭiya makaṉum, tuṟavu pūṇṭiruntavaṉumākiya pōtitarmaṉ eṉpavaṉ         520-ām varuṣam, intōṉēṣyā vaḻiyākac cīṉāvukkuc ceṉṟu, aṅku appoḻutu āṇṭu koṇṭirunta vū ṭī maṉṉaṉuṭiya 1 mariyātiyiyum ātaraviyum peṟṟāṉ. ivaṉ ceṉṟataṟkup piṟakutāṉ, cīṉāvil putta matattiṟku atika pirapalam ēṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum, naṟkatiyaṭivataṟkut tiyāṉa mārkkamē ciṟantateṉṟu ivaṉ pōtittu vantāṉeṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟum ivaṉ, anēka aṟputaṅkaḷic ceytu kāṭṭiyatākavum, ivaṉ koḷki jappāṉ vari ceṉṟu paraviyatākavum cīṉa varalāṟukaḷ ivaṉip pukaḻntu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉ nūṟṟimpatu varuṣamvari vāḻntāṉeṉpar.
vinīta ruci eṉṟa oru tamiḻakattup pirāmaṇaṉ 582-ām varuṣam cīṉā pōyc cērntu, aṅku iraṇṭu puttamata kirantaṅkaḷic cīṉa moḻiyil moḻi peyarttāṉ; pōtitarmaṉuṭiya tiyāṉa mārkkam parava vēṇṭumeṉpataṟkāka atika muyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭāṉ.
pōtiruci  eṉṟa oru paṇṭitaṉ 693-ām varuṣam cīṉāvi aṭintu, aṅku aracaṉuṭiya aṉpiyum ātaraviyum peṟṟu, anēka puttamata kirantaṅkaḷic cīṉa moḻiyil moḻi peyart tāṉ. putta matattiṉ oru kiḷiyākiya makāyāṉattiṉ kōṭ pāṭukaḷ cilavaṟṟi vivarikkum ratṉakūṭam eṉṟa oru nūli ivaṉ moḻi peyarttuc collikkoṇṭu pōkiṟapōtu, ati aracaṉē taṉ kiyāl eḻuti vantāṉeṉpar. itiliruntu, ivaṉukku aracaviyil irunta matippu oruvāṟu pulaṉākiṟatu. ivaṉ nūṟṟimpattāṟāvatu vayatu vari vāḻntāṉeṉṟum, kaṭici kālattil aimpattintu nāṭkaḷ uṇṇāviratamiruntu amiti yāka uyir tuṟantāṉeṉṟum kūṟuvar.
kāñcipurattil iraṇṭāvatu naracimma varmaṉ eṉṟa pallava maṉṉaṉ āṇṭa kālattil (695-722) avaṉuṭiya  naṉmatippip peṟṟirunta vajrapōti eṉṟa aṟiñaṉ, 720-ām varuṣam kāṇṭṭaṉ nakaratti aṭintu , anēka puttamata kirantaṅkaḷic cīṉa moḻiyil moḻi peyarttuk koṭuttāṉ.  intiyāvi liruntu ēṟkaṉavē ceṉṟirunta putta matat tuṟavikaḷ palar, ivaṉuṭiya cīṭarkaḷāyiṉar. ivaṉ 732-ām varuṭam lōyāṅ eṉṟa nakarattil pūtavuṭali viṭuttāṉ
pāratvāja kōttirattic cērnta pōticēṉaṉ eṉṟa oru tamiḻakattup pirāmaṇaṉ 733-ām varuṣam cīṉāviyaṭintu, aṅkuc ciṟitu kālam taṅkiviṭṭup piṟaku jappāṉukkuc ceṉṟāṉ. aṅku 750-ām varuṣam putta maṭālayaṅkaḷ aṉittiṟkum talivaṉākat terinteṭukkappaṭṭup perumatippuṭaṉ vāḻntu vantāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam tamiḻakattiliruntu palar ceṉṟatu pōl, cīṉāvi liruntum palar tamiḻakattiṟku vantirukkiṟārkaḷ. āyiṉum, ivarkaḷil oruvaṉit tavira maṟṟavarkaḷip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ atikamākat teriyavilli.  inta oruvaṉtāṉ ēṟkaṉavē collap peṟṟa hyūṉ tsāṅ. ivaṉ, pāhiyāṉippōl, vaṭa intiyā vōṭu taṉ yāttiriyi muṭittukkoṇṭuviṭāmal tamiḻakat tilum yāttiri ceytāṉ. kaliṅkam, āntiram, cōḻanāṭu, pallava rājyam, pāṇṭiya tēcam, koṅkaṇam ākiya pakutikaḷip paṟṟit taṉakkut terintavaṟṟi, taṉ yāttiri nūlil terivittirukkiṟāṉ.
pallava rājyattiṟku ivaṉ pōnta pōtu, mutalāvatu naracimmavarmaṉuṭiya āṭci (630 mutal 668-ām varuṣam vari) naṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭiruntatu. intap pēraracaṉtāṉ, makāpali puratti oru ciṟpakkaḷañciyamākac ceytavaṉ; atumaṭṭu maṉṟu; talinakaramāṉa kāñcipuratti aṟivilum tiruvilum ōṅkiyirukkum paṭi ceytavaṉ.
hyūṉ tsāṅ ēṟakkuṟiya 640-ām varuṣam, kāñcipuram pōntu, naracimmavarmaṉāl kuravikkappeṟṟāṉ. taṉṉuṭiya  yāttiri nūlil, kāñcipurattip paṟṟiyum, toṇṭi maṇṭalattip paṟṟiyum varuṇittirukkiṟāṉ. kāñcipuram ap poḻutu ēṟakkuṟiya oṉpatari catura kilō mīṭṭar (āṟu catura mil) vitīraṇamuṭiyatāka iruntateṉṟum, aṅkuc cumār nūṟu putta maṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum ivaṟṟil patiṉā yirattukku mēṟpaṭṭa piṭcukkaḷ vacittukkoṇṭiruntār kaḷeṉṟum, putta maṭaṅkaḷ tavira, vēṟu pala matattiṉaruṭiya kōyilkaḷum aṅku iruntaṉaveṉṟum  ivaṉ kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. maṟṟum ivaṉ, toṇṭi maṇṭalattiluḷḷavarkaḷ aṟivukkup perumatippuk koṭukkiṟārkaḷeṉṟu taṉ nūlil kūṟiyiruppatu toṇṭi naṉṉāṭu cāṉṟōruṭittu eṉṉum cemmoḻikku  araṇ ceyvatāyirukkiṟatu. pāṇṭiya tēcattip paṟṟi ivaṉ kuṟippiṭukiṟa pōtu, atu, kaṭal muttukkaḷ niṟinta oru kaḷañciya meṉṟum,  aṅkuḷḷa  makkaḷ viyāpārattil kitērntavarkaḷā yirukkiṟārkaḷeṉṟum terivikkiṟāṉ.
hyūṉ tsāṅkukkup piṟaku tamiḻakattiṟku vanta yāttiri karkaḷ, ēṟkaṉavē cuṭṭik kāṭṭiyapaṭi peyaraḷavil maṭṭumē namakku aṟimukamā kiṟārkaḷ. miṅ yuvāṉ, 1 ṭāvō liṉ,2  vū hiṅ3 ippaṭic cilar vantu pōṉārkaḷ. 
iṉi, intiyāvukkum, cīṉāvukkum irunta vēṟu vakiyāṉa toṭarpukaḷ cilavaṟṟip paṟṟic ciṟitu kūṟuvōm
viyāpāra nimittamākavum, puttamata piracāra nimittamākavum intiyāviṉ palvēṟu pakutikaḷiliruntu ceṉṟavarkaḷ mūlamākac cīṉāvil, intiyāviṉ kalāccāramum metumetuvākap pukuntu paraviyatu. pārata nāṭṭil paṉṉeṭuṅ kālamāka vaḷarntu vanta nuṇ kalikaḷ, ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, vāḻkki niyatikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ cāyal, cīṉa camutāya vāḻvil pala tuṟikaḷilum paṭintu, putiya kalip paṭippukkaḷ tōṉṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāyiruntaṉa.
ciṟpak kaliyilum ōviyakkaliyilum intiyarkaḷ kiyāṇṭa muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi, cīṉāvil puttar uruvaṅkaḷ pala camikkap paṭṭaṉa; putta maṭaṅkaḷil anēka vaṇṇa ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. intak kalikaḷil vallunarkaḷāṉa intiyar palar cīṉā vukkuc ceṉṟu peyarum pukaḻum peṟṟārkaḷ. cīṉakkaliñarkaḷ pōṟṟum cākkiyaputtaṉ, puttakīrtti, kumārapōti mutaliyōr intiyāviliruntu ceṉṟa ōviyakkali nipuṇarkaḷē.
intiyāviṉ icik kalikkuc  cīṉāvil atika celvākku ēṟpaṭṭiruntatu. 581-ām varuṣam icip pulavar palarik koṇṭa oru kōṣṭi intiyāviliruntu cīṉāvukkuc ceṉṟatu. cīṉa maṉṉarkaḷ, intiya ici vakikaḷil layittuppōy, ivarkaḷukkup pērātaravu tantārkaḷ.
veku kālattiṟku muntiyē intiyarkaḷ kaṇṭu teḷintu vittirunta kaṇita cātiram, vāṉa cātiram, vittiya cātiram mutaliyaṉa, cīṉāvil nalla celvākkup peṟṟaṉa. pañcāṅkaṅkaḷ kaṇikka, cīṉa maṉṉarkaḷ avvappoḻutu niyamittu vanta nipuṇar kuḻuvil, intiyāviliruntu ceṉṟa vāṉacātirikaḷ palar iṭam  peṟṟaṉar. intiyāviṉ navakkiraka muṟi cīṉāvil piṉpaṟṟap paṭṭatu. vāṉacātirattip paṟṟiyum kaṇita cātirattip paṟṟiyum vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa nūlkaḷ pala cīṉamoḻiyil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē vittiya nūlkaḷ palavum moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa. ki. pi. aintāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu patiṉō rāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu vari, inta moḻipeyarppup paṇi tīviramāka naṭi peṟṟatu.
nīṇṭa āyuḷi aḷikkavalla kāyakalpa cikicci peṟa, cīṉa maṉṉar palar peritum virumpiṉar. itaṟkāka intiyāviliruntu anēka nipuṇarkaḷ varavaḻikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil oruvaṉ peyar nārāyaṇacuvāmi.1 intiyāviliruntu apūrva mūlikikaḷic cēkarittukkoṇṭu vara cīṉa aracāṅka uttiyōkatar cilarum aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam palavakiyāṉa toṭarpukaḷ, cīṉāvukkum intiyāvukkumiṭiyē neṭuṅkālamāka iruntu vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi toṭarntum nilittum irukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu, ellōrum iṉpuṟṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu, niṉikkiṉṟavar kaḷuṭiya peru viruppam.


1842	-	nāṉkiṅ uṭaṉpaṭikki; apiṉi viyāpāram kāraṇamākac cīṉāvukkum piriṭṭaṉukkum ēṟpaṭṭatu. (29-8-1842).
1843	-	pōk oppantam; mēṟpaṭi nāṉkiṅ uṭaṉpaṭikkiyit toṭarntu, viyāpāra vitikaḷ, cuṅka varikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi nirṇayikkiṉṟa muṟiyil cīṉāvukkum piriṭṭaṉukkum ēṟpaṭṭatu. (8-10-1843)
1844	-	mēṟpaṭi oppantatti aṉucarittu cīṉā vukkum amerikkā vukkum oppantam. (3-7-1844)
1987	-	mēṟpaṭi rītiyil cīṉāvukkum nārvē - vīṭaṉukkum oppantam (20-3-1847)
		mēṟpaṭi rītiyil cīṉāvukkum pirāṉsukkum oppantam.               (24-10-1847).
1858	-	ṭīṇṭsiṉ oppantam; viyāpāram mutaliya tuṟikaḷil anniyarkaḷiṉ celvākki atikarittuk koṭukkiṉṟa muṟiyil, 13-6-1858-l cīṉāvukkum ruṣyāvukkum, 18-6-1858l cīṉāvukkum amerikkāvukkum, 26-6-1858-l cīṉāvukkum piriṭṭaṉukkum, 27-6-1858-l cīṉāvukkum pirāṉsukkum taṉit taṉiyāka oppantam ēṟpaṭṭatu.
1871	-	pōrmōsā tīvil nikaḻnta oru campavattiṉ viḷivākac cīṉāvukkum jappāṉukkum ōr oppantam ēṟpaṭṭatu. ituvē iraṇṭu nāṭukaḷukkum mutaṉ mutalāka ēṟpaṭṭa oppantam
1876	-	koriyāviṉ taṉittuvatti aṅkīkarik kiṉṟa muṟiyil, jappāṉukkum koriyā vukkum ōr oppantam ēṟpaṭṭatu.                (26-2-1876).
	-  	cēpū oppantam; mārkēri eṉṟa piriṭṭiṣ tāṉikaṉ yuṉṉāṉ mākāṇattil koli ceyyappaṭṭataṉ viḷi vākac cīṉāvukkum piriṭṭaṉukkum ēṟpaṭṭatu. (13-9-1876)
1895	-	ṣimōṉōseki oppantam; koriyā campantamāka cīṉāvukkum jappāṉukkum ēṟpaṭṭatu. (17-4-1895)
1901	-	pāksar oppantam; pāksar kalakattiṉ viḷivāka, cīṉāvukkum, piriṭṭaṉ, pirāṉ, jermaṉi mutaliya vallaracu kaḷukkum ēṟpaṭṭatu. (7-9-1901)
1902	-	āṅkilō-jappāṉiya oppantam; cīṉāvil piriṭṭaṉiṉ urimikaḷum, koriyāvil jappāṉiṉ urimikaḷum muṟiyē pātu kākkappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉpatiyoṭṭi ēṟpaṭṭatu. (30-1-1902).
1905	-	pōrṭmavut oppantam. ruṣya -jappāṉiya yuttattiṉ viḷivāka ēṟpaṭṭatu. (5-9-1905)
1915	-	jappāṉiṉ irupattōru kōrikkikaḷ campantamākac cīṉāvukkum jappāṉukkum ōr oppantam ēṟpaṭṭatu.                   (25-5-1915)
1917	-	ṣāṇṭuṅ  tīpakaṟpam campantamāka jappāṉum piriṭṭaṉum oru rakaciya oppantam ceytu koṇṭaṉa. (16-2-1917) itē pirakāram, jappāṉ, pirāṉsuṭaṉ oru rakaciya oppantamum            (1-3-1917) ruṣyāvuṭaṉ oru rakaciya oppantamum                                 (5-3-1917)ceytu koṇṭatu.
1922	-	vāṣiṅkṭaṉ oppantam; ṣāṇṭuṅ tīpakaṟpam campantamākac cīṉāvukkum jappāṉukkum vāṣiṅkṭaṉ nakarattil ēṟpaṭṭatu. (4-2-1922).
	-	oṉpatu vallaracukaḷ oppantam; cīṉāviṉ cutantiratti aṅkīkarikkiṉṟa muṟiyil piriṭṭaṉ, pirāṉ mutaliya oṉpatu val laracukaḷ ōr oppantam ceytu koṇṭaṉa. vāṣiṅkṭaṉ nakarattil inta oppantam kiyeḻuttiṭap peṟṟatu. (6-2-1922).
1933	-	ṭaṅkkū oppantam; mañcūriyā viṣayamākac cīṉāvukkum jappāṉukkum ēṟpaṭṭatu. (31-5-1933)
